        # Implementation Plan: M7B — Architectural Rewrites (Overlay Bridge + Settings Bootstrap Collapse)

## Overview

M7A shipped the mechanical cleanup slice (WP1/WP2/WP6/WP7). M7B lands the **architectural rewrites** that M7A explicitly deferred via its scope firewall: WP3 (overlay bridge `open` correctness), WP4 (collapse `useAutoSaveSettings` bootstrap effects), WP5 (port the same collapse to `useLastUsedEditSettings`). These are the slices that exit the M1–M6 mirror pattern by removing RQ-result identity from effect deps and by gating overlay registration on the real `open` state rather than on ref attachment.

Working branch: `megaplan/m7-state-cleanup` (worktree `/private/tmp/reigh-chain-workspace/reigh-app`).

Baseline repo state (confirmed via inspection):
- `overlayBridge.tsx:25-30` — `UseOverlayBridgeOptions` has no `open`; `syncRegistration` at `:136-161` pops only on zero elements (not on close).
- `overlayStack.ts:88-101` — `isOverlayActuallyOpen` still present (DOM-reading workaround that has to go).
- `overlayStack.ts:103-112` — `getTopModalOverlay` still calls `isOverlayActuallyOpen`.
- 8 Content wrappers call `useOverlayBridge({...})` without forwarding `open`:
  - `dialog.tsx:68` (DialogContent), `alert-dialog.tsx:56` (AlertDialogContent), `lightbox.tsx:38` (LightboxDialogPortal), `select.tsx:198` (SelectContent), `popover.tsx:51` (PopoverContent), `dropdown-menu.tsx:140` (DropdownMenuContent) + `:83` (DropdownMenuSubContent), `hover-card.tsx:40` (HoverCardContent), `tooltip.tsx:49` (TooltipContent). Only non-test callers.
- `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:236-240` — `lastBootstrapRef` alive. Two bootstrap effects at `:280-307` and `:309-359`; deps include `authoritativeSettings` (the RQ result — the exact violation to fix). deepEqual guards at `:291` and `:323`.
- `useLastUsedEditSettings.ts:155-179` — effect deps include `dbSettings`; deepEqual guard at `:165-172`. `useToolSettings` returns `settings: T | undefined` (confirmed at `useToolSettings.ts` :146 / :154), so the stable key must be `dbSettings !== undefined`, not `!== null`.
- Toolchain status: `node_modules/@base-ui/react` is an empty directory; `node_modules/.bin/{tsc,vitest}` absent. Same environment M7A ran under — runtime verification will be `status=skipped` / `info`, static evidence carries the gate.
- `docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md` exists (20 lines, from M7A T7). Per M7 v3 Step 14, M7B adds a "Consequences of one-shot bootstrap" section so the doc matches the new invariant.

Invariants enforced by this plan (from M7 v3, still in force):
1. **No RQ result in `useEffect` deps that calls a zustand setter.** WP4/WP5 effects depend only on entity id + stable scalars (`hasShotSettings`, `isCustomMode`, `isEntityValid`, `authoritativeIsLoading`, `hasPersistedData`). RQ results are held via refs and read inside the effect body.
2. **Overlay `open` is required on `UseOverlayBridgeOptions`**, and Content wrappers self-source it. Callers of `<Dialog>`, `<Popover>`, `<Tooltip>`, etc. are NOT modified.
3. **`useToolSettings` public API untouched** (no `dataUpdatedAt` on the return).
4. **Scope firewall**: do not touch WP1/WP2/WP6/WP7 surface already landed by M7A. Do not revert `didBootstrapRef`, the inlined panes store, the stripped diagnostic logs, or `state_ownership.md` content beyond the explicit one-section extension.

## Phase 1: Overlay Bridge Correction (WP3)

### Step 1: Pre-check Base UI, then adopt uniform MutationObserver fallback (`package.json`, `node_modules/@base-ui/react`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** `ls node_modules/@base-ui/react` and record the output. Confirm the directory is empty (matches M7A baseline).
2. **Do not** attempt `npm install` in the sandbox; M7A proved the install path is not available and the fallback was already pre-approved.
3. **Record** an executor note: "Base UI sources unavailable — classifying all 8 primitives as (c) MutationObserver fallback per M7 v3 Phase-2 decision." This uniform classification is load-bearing for Step 3; no per-primitive `useXRootContext` wiring is attempted.

### Step 2: Make `open` required on `useOverlayBridge` and gate registration on it (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/overlayBridge.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `open: boolean` as a required field on `UseOverlayBridgeOptions` at `overlayBridge.tsx:25-30`.
2. **Track** `open` via a ref on every render (same pattern as `typeRef`/`modalRef`):
   ```ts
   const openRef = React.useRef(options.open);
   openRef.current = options.open;
   ```
3. **Modify** `syncRegistration` at `:136-161` to return (and pop if registered) whenever `!openRef.current || elements.length === 0`. Keep the existing pop/push/update control flow intact for the "open && elements present" path.
4. **Add** `options.open` to the `useEffect` at `:186-188` that re-runs `syncRegistration` when `open|modal|restoreFocusTo|type` change, so transitions of `open` fire the pop.
5. **Leave** unmount cleanup at `:190-204` untouched — it still unconditionally pops if registered.

### Step 3: Wire 8 Content wrappers to self-source `open` (MutationObserver fallback) (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/{dialog,alert-dialog,lightbox,select,popover,dropdown-menu,hover-card,tooltip}.tsx`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** a small co-located helper `useOpenFromDataState(node: HTMLElement | null): boolean` — state + `useEffect` that attaches a `MutationObserver` watching `attributeFilter: ['data-state', 'data-open']` on `node`, re-reading with `state === 'open' || element.hasAttribute('data-open')`, defaulting to `false` when `node` is `null`. Put this in a new helper module `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/useOpenFromDataState.ts` so all 8 wrappers share one implementation.
2. **Update each Content wrapper** so the popup ref is first captured into a local `popupElRef` (via `composeRefs`), the helper reads from it, and the resulting `open` is passed into `useOverlayBridge({ type, modal, open, ... })`. Order of hooks must be: `useRef` → `useOpenFromDataState` → `useOverlayBridge` → `useOverlayLayer`, so `open` is available at the bridge call. Illustrative shape (DialogContent):
   ```tsx
   const popupElRef = React.useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
   const open = useOpenFromDataState(popupElRef.current);
   const bridge = useOverlayBridge({ type: 'dialog', modal: true, open });
   const layer = useOverlayLayer(bridge.handle.id);
   const popupRef = React.useMemo(
     () => composeRefs(
       ref,
       (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => { popupElRef.current = node; },
       (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => bridge.registerElement('popup', node),
     ),
     [bridge, ref],
   );
   ```
   Attach the same `popupElRef` write to the existing popup `ref` composition; **do not** add a second DOM traversal or a separate `useState` in each wrapper — `useOpenFromDataState` owns the state.
3. **Apply the same pattern** to AlertDialogContent (`alert-dialog.tsx:56`), LightboxDialogPortal (`lightbox.tsx:38`, reads `modal` from LightboxDialogModalContext — keep that, just add `open`), SelectContent (`select.tsx:198`), PopoverContent (`popover.tsx:51`), DropdownMenuContent (`dropdown-menu.tsx:140`), DropdownMenuSubContent (`dropdown-menu.tsx:83`), HoverCardContent (`hover-card.tsx:40`), TooltipContent (`tooltip.tsx:49`). For wrappers whose `modal` comes from a context (`select`/`popover`/`dropdown-menu`/`lightbox`), preserve that wiring unchanged.
4. **Do NOT modify** callers of `<Dialog>`, `<Popover>`, `<Tooltip>`, `<HoverCard>`, `<Select>`, `<DropdownMenu>`, `<AlertDialog>`, `<Lightbox>`. Raw-Root aliases at `tooltip.tsx:15` and `hover-card.tsx:8` stay as-is.

### Step 4: Delete `isOverlayActuallyOpen` and revert `getTopModalOverlay` (`src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Delete** the `isOverlayActuallyOpen` function at `overlayStack.ts:88-101` — no caller remains once Step 2/3 land (the bridge now gates on `open`).
2. **Revert** `getTopModalOverlay` at `:103-112` to a simple reverse-iteration: `return overlays[i]` for the first `overlay.modal === true`. No DOM reads.
3. **Verify** no other file references `isOverlayActuallyOpen` after deletion.

### Step 5: Update overlay tests for the new contract (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/*.test.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Find** every test that calls `useOverlayBridge` directly (per the prior audit: `overlayBridge.test.tsx` at `:75`, `:198`). Update each call site to pass `open: true` (or a controlled flag) as a required option.
2. **Leave** tests that render the `<Content>` wrappers (`lightbox.test.tsx`, `dropdown-menu.test.tsx`, `hover-card.test.tsx`, `tooltip.test.tsx`, `overlaySequences.test.tsx`) unchanged in their public shape — the wrappers self-source `open` now — but re-read each one to confirm no assertion depended on `isOverlayActuallyOpen` semantics.
3. **Add** `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/__tests__/select.closed.test.tsx` asserting that mounting `<Select defaultValue="">` with a `<SelectContent>` **without opening** leaves `document.body.style.pointerEvents` at its default and registers no modal overlay entry. This is the regression-reproduction test for the original pointer-events stranding.

## Phase 2: Settings Bootstrap Collapse (WP4)

### Step 6: Collapse `useAutoSaveSettings` bootstrap effects (`src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** two refs at the top of the hook body (near the other runtime refs, above `activeEntityRef`):
   ```ts
   const authoritativeSettingsRef = useRef(authoritativeSettings);
   authoritativeSettingsRef.current = authoritativeSettings;
   const bootstrapDataRef = useRef(bootstrapData);
   bootstrapDataRef.current = bootstrapData;
   ```
2. **Delete** `lastBootstrapRef` at `:236-240` and every reference (`:321-341`).
3. **Collapse** the two bootstrap effects at `:280-307` and `:309-359` into one. Branch on `isCustomMode` inside the effect body; read seeds via refs:
   ```ts
   useEffect(() => {
     if (!isEntityValid || authoritativeIsLoading || authoritativeError) return;
     if (isCustomMode) {
       const seed = bootstrapDataRef.current;
       if (!seed) return;
       bootstrapEntity({ entityId: activeEntityId, db: null, lastUsed: cloneValue(seed) });
       return;
     }
     const seed = cloneValue(authoritativeSettingsRef.current ?? defaults);
     bootstrapEntity({
       entityId: activeEntityId,
       db: hasShotSettings ? seed : null,
       lastUsed: hasShotSettings ? null : seed,
     });
   }, [activeEntityId, hasShotSettings, isCustomMode, isEntityValid, authoritativeIsLoading, authoritativeError, bootstrapEntity, defaults]);
   ```
   Note: `authoritativeSettings` and `bootstrapData` are **not** in deps. `store` is not in deps (the effect no longer reads `store.getState().entities`).
4. **Delete** the `deepEqual(currentEntity.cleanSnapshot, ...)` guards at the old `:291` and `:323` sites (dropped as part of the collapse).
5. **Preserve** untouched: the `reloadStoredEntity` effect at `:272-278`, the `beforeunload` effect, the mount/unmount entity flush effect. `useToolSettings` invocation and public return shape are untouched.
6. **Add** an inline comment above the collapsed effect explaining the invariant — one short line: `// Per state_ownership.md: RQ result object identity is intentionally absent from deps; consumers needing fresh authoritative data read useToolSettings() directly.`

### Step 7: `useAutoSaveSettings` bootstrap idempotency test (`src/shared/settings/hooks/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Assert** initial bootstrap fires exactly once when `authoritativeIsLoading` flips true→false for an entity.
2. **Assert** 5 rerenders with identity-stable props (no dep change) fire zero additional bootstraps.
3. **Assert** changing `activeEntityId` (via `shotId` or `projectId` change) triggers a second bootstrap.
4. **Assert (invariant)**: a same-tab RQ refetch — new `authoritativeSettings` object identity while `authoritativeIsLoading` stays false — does NOT trigger re-bootstrap. Comment explains this is the deliberate one-shot-bootstrap tradeoff.

## Phase 3: Settings Bootstrap Collapse (WP5)

### Step 8: Port the collapse to `useLastUsedEditSettings` (`src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLastUsedEditSettings.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** a ref for `dbSettings` right after the `useToolSettings` call:
   ```ts
   const dbSettingsRef = useRef(dbSettings);
   dbSettingsRef.current = dbSettings;
   ```
2. **Compute** a stable boolean key that handles the `T | undefined → object` transition correctly (since `useToolSettings` returns `T | undefined`):
   ```ts
   const hasPersistedData = dbSettings !== undefined;
   ```
3. **Replace** the effect at `:155-179` with a collapsed form keyed on `[entityId, hasPersistedData, bootstrapEntity]`, reading the seed from `dbSettingsRef` inside the body, and dropping the deepEqual guard:
   ```ts
   useEffect(() => {
     if (!entityId) return;
     const localSettings = readLocalStorageSettings(entityId);
     const db = dbSettingsRef.current;
     const dbSnapshot = db !== undefined ? { ...localSettings, ...db } : null;
     bootstrapEntity({ entityId, db: dbSnapshot, lastUsed: localSettings });
   }, [entityId, hasPersistedData, bootstrapEntity]);
   ```
4. **Delete** the `hasPersistedData === (dbSnapshot !== null)` and `deepEqual(currentEntity.cleanSnapshot, nextSeed)` block at `:165-172`. Keep the preceding `reloadStoredEntity` effect at `:147-153` unchanged.

### Step 9: `useLastUsedEditSettings` bootstrap idempotency test (`src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/__tests__/useLastUsedEditSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Assert** the `undefined → object` transition (RQ first load) fires bootstrap with `db: {...}`.
2. **Assert** 5 identity-stable rerenders fire zero additional bootstraps.
3. **Assert** `entityId` change fires a fresh bootstrap with the new entity's local seed.
4. **Assert (invariant)**: a post-load content change in `dbSettings` where `hasPersistedData` remains `true` does NOT re-bootstrap.

## Phase 4: Documentation (WP3/WP4/WP5 invariant)

### Step 10: Extend `state_ownership.md` with the one-shot-bootstrap consequence (`docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Read** the current doc (created by M7A, 20 lines, < 80 line budget). Keep Purpose + Source-of-truth table + three do-nots + `git log` pointer intact.
2. **Add** a single new section `## Consequences of one-shot bootstrap` (≈10 lines) stating: once `bootstrapEntity` runs for an entity, same-tab RQ refetches do NOT re-bootstrap `cleanSnapshot`; consumers needing fresh authoritative data must read `useToolSettings()` directly. Point to the collapsed effects in `useAutoSaveSettings.ts` and `useLastUsedEditSettings.ts` as the reference implementation.
3. **Verify** `wc -l docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md` < 80. No new `.md` files — this extends the one M7A explicitly requested. Do not add an overlay-bridge section (no new doc requested for WP3).

## Phase 5: Verification

### Step 11: Static evidence gates
**Scope:** Small
1. `rg 'isOverlayActuallyOpen' src` → empty.
2. `rg 'lastBootstrapRef' src` → empty.
3. `rg 'cleanSnapshot' src --glob '!*.test.*'` → only `createEntityStore.ts` internals (no prod hits in `useAutoSaveSettings.ts` or `useLastUsedEditSettings.ts`).
4. `rg 'dataUpdatedAt' src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts` → empty (public API untouched).
5. `rg 'open:\\s*boolean' src/shared/components/ui/overlay/overlayBridge.tsx` → ≥ 1 (`open` is now in `UseOverlayBridgeOptions`).
6. `rg 'useOpenFromDataState' src/shared/components/ui/overlay` → ≥ 9 (1 definition + 8 wrapper import sites). DropdownMenuContent + DropdownMenuSubContent both count against `dropdown-menu.tsx`, so total imports ≥ 8 files when counting unique wrappers (dialog, alert-dialog, lightbox, select, popover, dropdown-menu, hover-card, tooltip).
7. `rg 'didBootstrapRef' src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineState.ts` ≥ 2 (unchanged from M7A — scope-firewall check).
8. `rg 'usePaneLockPolicyState' src` empty (unchanged — scope-firewall check).
9. `wc -l docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md` still < 80 after Step 10's addition.
10. `rg 'Consequences of one-shot bootstrap' docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md` ≥ 1.
Record all outputs in `.megaplan/plans/m7b-architectural-rewrites/evidence_static.txt`.

### Step 12: Build + test verification (degraded-path aware)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Check** `ls node_modules/.bin/tsc node_modules/.bin/vitest`. If both present (unlikely per M7A baseline): run `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npx vitest run src/shared/components/ui/overlay src/shared/settings/hooks src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks`, then the full `npm test`.
2. **If** the binaries are absent (expected case): emit an explicit executor deviation. Mark the tsc/vitest success criteria `status=skipped`. Do NOT claim tests passed. Static evidence from Step 11 carries the verification gate.
3. **Runtime smoke** (travel-between-images load not blocked, body pointer-events not stranded on unopened Select, media-lightbox edit lastUsed still hydrates): record as `info` priority — cannot be driven from this sandbox, requires human verification post-merge.

## Execution Order
1. Phase 1 Step 1 (Base UI pre-check) — emit deviation up front so downstream steps know fallback is in force.
2. Phase 1 Steps 2 → 3 → 4 → 5 — land `open` on the bridge, wire wrappers, then delete the DOM-reading workaround and update tests (tests last so the compile surface settles).
3. Phase 2 Steps 6 → 7 — collapse `useAutoSaveSettings` then add its bootstrap test.
4. Phase 3 Steps 8 → 9 — port to `useLastUsedEditSettings` then add its bootstrap test.
5. Phase 4 Step 10 — extend `state_ownership.md`.
6. Phase 5 Steps 11 → 12 — static gates first (cheap, always run), toolchain gates last (may be skipped).

## Validation Order
1. Per-step: file-targeted `rg` (e.g., after Step 4, `rg 'isOverlayActuallyOpen' src` empty).
2. After Phase 1: run `npx vitest run src/shared/components/ui/overlay` if the toolchain is available.
3. After Phase 3: full `npx tsc --noEmit` (WP4/WP5 share `useToolSettings` type surface; type check catches any drift from the hasPersistedData refactor).
4. After Phase 4: Step 11 static evidence sweep.
5. Step 12 toolchain verification last; skip with documented deviation if `tsc`/`vitest` binaries are absent.
6. Runtime smoke deferred to human (info).

      "id": "DEBT-038",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the current callers of `isrecord` from `llm/messages.ts`. `index.ts` uses it to validate `rawsession`, `db.ts` uses it on db rows, `tool-calls.ts` uses it on parsed tool-call payloads and nested `function` objects, and `selectedclips.ts` uses it while normalizing attachments, so preserving that import contract matters to four different call paths.",

    {
      "id": "DEBT-095",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the current `commitdata()` and history pipeline treat each `applyedit()` as its own commit boundary. because step 16 proposes a post-commit reconcile edit after deletions, the actual callers here matter: without `skiphistory` or transaction coalescing, one user action will produce two history events and likely two saves.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-096",

      "occurrence_count": 2,

    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-111",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual commit/history callers around step 18. `commitdata()` emits `beforecommit` and schedules save on every edit, and `usetimelinehistory.onbeforecommit()` pushes undo entries per commit boundary, so a reconcile-after-delete `applyedit` still fragments one user action into multiple history/save operations unless the plan also changes those caller contracts.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {

      {
        "id": "DEBT-041",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately.",

        "plan_ids": [
          "cleanup-and-quality-pass-on-20260404-0138"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-055",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-audio-reactive-effects-to-20260406-0440"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-056",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-audio-reactive-effects-to-20260406-0440"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-057",

        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-audio-reactive-effects-to-20260406-0440"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-070",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked revised step 2.4 against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useclipresize.ts:76-100`. the plan still says to clamp audio by writing back to `action.end`, but the current hook treats the callback `action` as input data and then creates fresh row actions from it. mutating that caller-owned object is unnecessary and risks coupling the resize hook to outside references instead of using a local `clampedend` variable.",

        "plan_ids": [

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-a-final-video-available-20260406-0801"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-077",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hand-built replacement clip against the existing insert path in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:201-252,262-271` and serialization in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts:210-216`. the revised `timelineeditor` sketch still bypasses current insertion invariants such as manual-track geometry defaults and explicit `cliporder` updates, so replacement clips on manual visual tracks will not be initialized the same way as other inserted video clips and may serialize in a different per-track order.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-a-final-video-available-20260406-0801"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-089",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,

        Idea:
/tmp/reigh-chain-workspace/ideas/M7B-architectural-rewrites.txt

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: M7B — Architectural Rewrites (Overlay Bridge + Settings Bootstrap Collapse)

## Overview

M7A landed the mechanical cleanup slice (WP1/WP2/WP6/WP7). M7B addresses the **architectural rewrites** deferred by M7A's scope firewall: WP3 (overlay bridge `open` correctness), WP4 (collapse `useAutoSaveSettings` bootstrap effects, keyed on `dataUpdatedAt`), WP5 (port the same collapse to `useLastUsedEditSettings`).

**Key correction from iteration 1.** The idea file for M7B explicitly specifies a `dataUpdatedAt`-keyed bootstrap effect where a **genuine React-Query refetch with new data DOES re-bootstrap `cleanSnapshot`**. Iteration 1 incorrectly inherited an earlier "one-shot bootstrap" decision from the prior M7 v3 iteration-3 (which had dropped `dataUpdatedAt`). This revision restores the `dataUpdatedAt`-keyed design. The invariant is:
- RQ result **object identity** stays out of effect deps.
- RQ's **scalar timestamp `dataUpdatedAt`** (a `number`) is in deps — it changes only on a genuine refetch.
- Consequence: same-entity refetches with new data re-bootstrap; stable-identity rerenders do not.

This requires surfacing `dataUpdatedAt: number` from `useToolSettings`. The M7 v3 "public API untouched" invariant is **explicitly relaxed** in M7B for this one field, because the idea file requires it.

Working branch: `megaplan/m7-state-cleanup` (worktree `/private/tmp/reigh-chain-workspace/reigh-app`).

Baseline state (confirmed via inspection):
- `overlayBridge.tsx:25-30` — `UseOverlayBridgeOptions` has no `open`; `syncRegistration` at `:136-161` only pops on zero elements.
- `overlayStack.ts:88-101` — `isOverlayActuallyOpen` DOM-reading workaround still present.
- `overlayStack.ts:103-112` — `getTopModalOverlay` still calls `isOverlayActuallyOpen`.
- 8 Content wrapper `useOverlayBridge({...})` call sites confirmed (dialog, alert-dialog, lightbox, select, popover, dropdown-menu [×2], hover-card, tooltip). `LightboxDialogPortal` is special: bridge is created in the **Portal** (`lightbox.tsx:33-54`) but the popup element only exists later in `LightboxDialogPopup` (`lightbox.tsx:76-93`) — M7B must move the bridge creation down to where the popup lives.
- `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:236-240` — `lastBootstrapRef`; two bootstrap effects at `:280-307` and `:309-359`; deps include `authoritativeSettings` (RQ result — the violation).
- `useLastUsedEditSettings.ts:155-179` — effect deps include `dbSettings`; deepEqual guard at `:165-172`.
- `useToolSettings` returns `settings: T | undefined` (confirmed). RQ's `dataUpdatedAt` is not currently surfaced.
- Toolchain: `node_modules/@base-ui/react` is empty; `tsc`/`vitest` binaries absent. Runtime verification is `info` in this sandbox; static-evidence gates carry the `must` tier.
- `docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md` exists (20 lines, from M7A T7).
- **Scope firewall** (deferred to M7C or later): `src/tools/join-clips/hooks/useJoinClipsSettings.ts:128-195` has the same RQ→zustand mirror pattern. Not touched by M7B — acknowledged for follow-up.

Invariants M7B enforces:
1. **RQ result object identity is not in effect deps.** Scalar `dataUpdatedAt` (a number) is the refetch key. RQ result objects held via ref, read inside the effect body.
2. **Overlay `open` is required on `UseOverlayBridgeOptions`.** Content wrappers self-source `open` via MutationObserver on `data-state`/`data-open`; the popup element is held in `useState` (not ref-only) so the helper is re-invoked after DOM attachment.
3. **Lightbox restructure**: bridge creation moves from `LightboxDialogPortal` to `LightboxDialogPopup` so the popup element and `open` signal live in the same component as the bridge.
4. **Scope firewall**: do not touch M7A's landed surface (`usePaneLockPolicyState` removal, `didBootstrapRef`, diagnostic log strip, inlined panes bootstrap). Do not touch `useJoinClipsSettings` in M7B.
5. **`useToolSettings` public API change is scoped to adding `dataUpdatedAt: number`.** No other shape changes.

## Phase 1: Overlay Bridge Correction (WP3)

### Step 1: Per-primitive classification of the 8 overlay wrappers (`node_modules/@base-ui/react`, the 8 wrapper files)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** `ls node_modules/@base-ui/react` and record output (expected: empty directory matching M7A baseline).
2. **Do not** run `npm install` — not available in sandbox.
3. **Classify each wrapper explicitly** per the idea file's required workflow, noting for each: (a) does it expose a controlled `open` via its local Root wrapper or a local context? (b) is a Base UI public `useXRootContext` hook reachable? (c) must we fall back to MutationObserver on `data-state`/`data-open`? Expected classification (verify with source):
   | Wrapper file | Root wrapper location | Controlled `open` available? | Final classification |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `dialog.tsx` | `:16` (`const Dialog = DialogPrimitive.Root`) | No — uncontrolled via Base UI | (c) MutationObserver |
   | `alert-dialog.tsx` | top of file | No | (c) MutationObserver |
   | `lightbox.tsx` | `:24-31` (wraps DialogPrimitive.Root) | No — internal to Base UI Dialog | (c) MutationObserver |
   | `select.tsx` | `:19-29` (local Select Root) | Root exposes `open`/`onOpenChange` props but app code doesn't drive them | (c) MutationObserver |
   | `popover.tsx` | `:10-17` (local Popover Root) | Same — Root props not app-driven | (c) MutationObserver |
   | `dropdown-menu.tsx` | `:13-20` (local DropdownMenu Root) | Same | (c) MutationObserver |
   | `hover-card.tsx` | wraps PreviewCardPrimitive | No | (c) MutationObserver |
   | `tooltip.tsx` | wraps TooltipPrimitive | No | (c) MutationObserver |
4. **Record** the classification in `.megaplan/plans/m7b-architectural-rewrites/evidence_classification.txt`. All 8 resolve to (c); `@base-ui/react` being empty makes (a) unreachable regardless, so even in a hydrated environment this classification is correct. An executor deviation is NOT required — this is the primary path.

### Step 2: Add `open` as required to `useOverlayBridge`, gate registration on it (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/overlayBridge.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `open: boolean` as required on `UseOverlayBridgeOptions` at `:25-30`.
2. **Track** `open` via a ref updated every render:
   ```ts
   const openRef = React.useRef(options.open);
   openRef.current = options.open;
   ```
3. **Modify** `syncRegistration` at `:136-161`: if `!openRef.current`, pop-if-registered and return. If `elements.length === 0`, same pop-if-registered path. Otherwise push/update as today.
4. **Re-fire** `syncRegistration` when `open` changes by including `options.open` in the deps of the `useEffect` at `:186-188` (alongside `modal`, `restoreFocusTo`, `type`).
5. **Leave** unmount cleanup at `:190-204` untouched.

### Step 3: Create shared MutationObserver helper using `useState` for the popup element (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/useOpenFromDataState.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** the file with:
   ```tsx
   import * as React from 'react';

   export function useOpenFromDataState(node: HTMLElement | null): boolean {
     const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
     React.useEffect(() => {
       if (!node) { setOpen(false); return; }
       const read = () => {
         const state = node.getAttribute('data-state');
         setOpen(state === 'open' || node.hasAttribute('data-open'));
       };
       read();
       const mo = new MutationObserver(read);
       mo.observe(node, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-state', 'data-open'] });
       return () => mo.disconnect();
     }, [node]);
     return open;
   }
   ```
   Key design note: the **caller holds `node` in `useState`** (not a bare ref), so setting it via a callback ref triggers rerender, which re-invokes this hook with the non-null `node` and the effect attaches the observer. This resolves the FLAG-001 / correctness-1 deadlock where a ref-only pattern never rerenders after attachment.

### Step 4: Wire 7 "local-popup" Content wrappers to self-source `open` (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/{dialog,alert-dialog,select,popover,dropdown-menu,hover-card,tooltip}.tsx`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Pattern for each wrapper** (DialogContent, AlertDialogContent, SelectContent, PopoverContent, DropdownMenuContent, DropdownMenuSubContent, HoverCardContent, TooltipContent):
   ```tsx
   const [popupEl, setPopupEl] = React.useState<HTMLElement | null>(null);
   const open = useOpenFromDataState(popupEl);
   const bridge = useOverlayBridge({ type, modal, open });
   const layer = useOverlayLayer(bridge.handle.id);
   const popupRef = React.useMemo(
     () => composeRefs(
       ref,
       (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => setPopupEl(node),
       (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => bridge.registerElement('popup', node),
     ),
     [bridge, ref],
   );
   ```
   The `setPopupEl` callback-ref write triggers rerender → `useOpenFromDataState(popupEl)` now sees the real node → MutationObserver attaches → reads `data-state` → `open` flips true on Base UI's open transition → `useOverlayBridge` re-runs `syncRegistration` (which registers the overlay because `openRef.current` is now true).
2. **Apply** the pattern to the 7 wrapper files: dialog.tsx (`:68`), alert-dialog.tsx (`:56`), select.tsx (`:198`), popover.tsx (`:51`), dropdown-menu.tsx (`:140` + `:83` — both DropdownMenuContent and DropdownMenuSubContent), hover-card.tsx (`:40`), tooltip.tsx (`:49`).
3. **Preserve** all existing `modal` derivation from context (SelectModalContext, PopoverModalContext, DropdownMenuModalContext).
4. **Do NOT modify** callers of the Root aliases (`<Dialog>`, `<Popover>`, etc.) outside `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/`. Raw-Root aliases at `tooltip.tsx:15` and `hover-card.tsx:8` stay.

### Step 5: Restructure `LightboxDialogPortal` / `LightboxDialogPopup` so the bridge lives where the popup lives (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/lightbox.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Inspect** `lightbox.tsx:33-54` (`LightboxDialogPortal` — creates bridge today) and `:76-93` (`LightboxDialogPopup` — owns the popup DOM node).
2. **Move** the `useOverlayBridge({ type: 'lightbox', modal, open })` call from `LightboxDialogPortal` down into `LightboxDialogPopup`. The Popup component owns the DOM element, so it can hold `popupEl` in `useState` and call `useOpenFromDataState` directly — same pattern as the other 7 wrappers.
3. **Keep** `LightboxDialogPortal` responsible only for the portal container + backdrop. Remove its `OverlayInstanceProvider` wiring if present and move it into `LightboxDialogPopup` so the provider wraps only the popup subtree where descendants need the handle.
4. **Preserve** `LightboxDialogModalContext` — `modal` still flows through it to the Popup.
5. **Verify** the lightbox test file (`lightbox.test.tsx`) still renders correctly after this structural change; update it if a test snapshots the portal/popup boundary.
6. **Do NOT** change the public surface (`<LightboxDialog>`, `<LightboxDialogPortal>`, `<LightboxDialogPopup>` exports). External callers are untouched.

### Step 6: Delete `isOverlayActuallyOpen` and revert `getTopModalOverlay` (`src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Delete** `isOverlayActuallyOpen` at `:88-101`.
2. **Revert** `getTopModalOverlay` at `:103-112` to a simple reverse-iteration: return the last overlay with `modal === true`. No DOM reads (no `getAttribute`, `hasAttribute`, `offsetParent`, `getClientRects`).
3. **Verify** no caller of `isOverlayActuallyOpen` remains.

### Step 7: Update overlay tests for the new contract (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/*.test.tsx` + new `__tests__/select.closed.test.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** every direct `useOverlayBridge` caller in tests (`overlayBridge.test.tsx:75`, `:198`) to pass `open: true` (or a controlled boolean driving both open and closed cases).
2. **Re-read** `lightbox.test.tsx`, `dropdown-menu.test.tsx`, `hover-card.test.tsx`, `tooltip.test.tsx`, `overlaySequences.test.tsx`. Update any assertion that depended on `isOverlayActuallyOpen` or on the old ref-only attachment ordering.
3. **Add** `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/__tests__/select.closed.test.tsx`: mount `<Select defaultValue=""><SelectContent>…</SelectContent></Select>` **without opening**, assert `document.body.style.pointerEvents` stays default (empty string), and assert `useOverlayStackApi().getState().overlays` contains no entry for this Select. This is the primary regression-reproduction test for the pointer-events stranding.

## Phase 2: Settings Bootstrap Collapse — WP4 (`useAutoSaveSettings`, `useToolSettings`)

### Step 8: Expose `dataUpdatedAt: number` from `useToolSettings` (`src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current return shape at the two overload sites (around `:146` and `:154`). The wrapped `useQuery` already computes `dataUpdatedAt` natively.
2. **Add** `dataUpdatedAt: number` to the returned object (from the `useQuery` result's `dataUpdatedAt`). Update both overload return types.
3. **Document** the addition with one-line inline comment: `// scalar refetch key for bootstrap effects; see state_ownership.md`.
4. **Leave** all other return fields (`settings`, `isLoading`, `error`, `update`, `hasShotSettings`, etc.) unchanged.

### Step 9: Collapse `useAutoSaveSettings` bootstrap effects, keyed on `dataUpdatedAt` (`src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Destructure** `dataUpdatedAt: authoritativeUpdatedAt` from the `useToolSettings` call alongside `settings`, `isLoading`, etc.
2. **Add** refs for the RQ result and bootstrap data (near the other runtime refs, above `activeEntityRef`):
   ```ts
   const authoritativeSettingsRef = useRef(authoritativeSettings);
   authoritativeSettingsRef.current = authoritativeSettings;
   const bootstrapDataRef = useRef(bootstrapData);
   bootstrapDataRef.current = bootstrapData;
   ```
3. **Delete** `lastBootstrapRef` at `:236-240` and every reference (`:321-341`).
4. **Collapse** the two bootstrap effects at `:280-307` (custom mode) and `:309-359` (react-query mode) into one. Branch on `isCustomMode` inside the body. Deps are **entity id + stable scalars**, with `authoritativeUpdatedAt` as the refetch key:
   ```ts
   // Bootstrap fires on entity-id / load / refetch transitions. RQ result object
   // identity is intentionally out of deps (see state_ownership.md); a genuine
   // refetch changes dataUpdatedAt and re-bootstraps cleanSnapshot with the new data.
   useEffect(() => {
     if (!isEntityValid || authoritativeIsLoading || authoritativeError) return;
     if (isCustomMode) {
       const seed = bootstrapDataRef.current;
       if (!seed) return;
       bootstrapEntity({ entityId: activeEntityId, db: null, lastUsed: cloneValue(seed) });
       return;
     }
     const seed = cloneValue(authoritativeSettingsRef.current ?? defaults);
     bootstrapEntity({
       entityId: activeEntityId,
       db: hasShotSettings ? seed : null,
       lastUsed: hasShotSettings ? null : seed,
     });
   }, [activeEntityId, hasShotSettings, isCustomMode, isEntityValid, authoritativeIsLoading, authoritativeError, authoritativeUpdatedAt, bootstrapEntity, defaults]);
   ```
   Note: `authoritativeSettings` and `bootstrapData` are **not** in deps (they're in refs). `authoritativeUpdatedAt` (scalar number) IS in deps — that's what preserves the required re-bootstrap-on-refetch behavior.
5. **For custom mode**, `bootstrapData` is held in `bootstrapDataRef`. If a caller like `useGenerationEditSettings` passes a new `bootstrapSettings` reference on subsequent renders, the ref updates (covered by Step 11 test), but a re-bootstrap only fires on `activeEntityId` change. That matches the one-shot-for-custom behavior documented in the idea file (custom mode has no RQ refetch, so no `dataUpdatedAt` dimension).
6. **Delete** the `deepEqual(currentEntity.cleanSnapshot, ...)` guards at the old `:291` and `:323` sites — the `dataUpdatedAt` key makes them redundant (same dataUpdatedAt ⇒ no re-run; different ⇒ intentional re-run).
7. **Preserve** the `reloadStoredEntity` effect at `:272-278`, the `beforeunload` effect, and the mount/unmount entity flush effect unchanged.

### Step 10: WP4 bootstrap idempotency + refetch test (`src/shared/settings/hooks/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Assert** initial bootstrap fires exactly once when `authoritativeIsLoading` flips true→false for an entity.
2. **Assert** 5 rerenders with identity-stable props (same `dataUpdatedAt`, same `authoritativeSettings` identity) fire zero additional bootstraps.
3. **Assert (critical)**: a genuine RQ refetch — `dataUpdatedAt` advances and `authoritativeSettings` contains new data — **does re-bootstrap** with the new authoritative snapshot (this is the idea-file-mandated invariant that iteration 1 broke).
4. **Assert** an identity-stable rerender that mutates `authoritativeSettings` object identity WITHOUT changing `dataUpdatedAt` does NOT re-bootstrap (the mirror-loop guard).
5. **Assert** changing `activeEntityId` (via `shotId`/`projectId`) fires a fresh bootstrap for the new entity.
6. **Assert** custom-mode caller shape (`useGenerationEditSettings`): a new `bootstrapData` object with same `entityId` does NOT re-bootstrap (custom mode is entity-keyed, not content-keyed — per idea-file contract). Add a comment that callers needing to re-seed an unpersisted custom entity must change the `entityId` or explicitly call a re-seed path.

## Phase 3: Settings Bootstrap Collapse — WP5 (`useLastUsedEditSettings`)

### Step 11: Port the `dataUpdatedAt`-keyed collapse to `useLastUsedEditSettings` (`src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLastUsedEditSettings.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Destructure** `dataUpdatedAt: dbUpdatedAt` from the `useToolSettings` call.
2. **Add** refs for `dbSettings` (and keep `fallbackLastUsed` as-is):
   ```ts
   const dbSettingsRef = useRef(dbSettings);
   dbSettingsRef.current = dbSettings;
   ```
3. **Compute** a stable scalar for presence (since `useToolSettings` returns `T | undefined`):
   ```ts
   const hasPersistedData = dbSettings !== undefined;
   ```
4. **Replace** the effect at `:155-179` with a collapsed form keyed on `[entityId, hasPersistedData, dbUpdatedAt, bootstrapEntity]`, reading seed from the ref inside the body, and dropping the deepEqual guard:
   ```ts
   useEffect(() => {
     if (!entityId) return;
     const localSettings = readLocalStorageSettings(entityId);
     const db = dbSettingsRef.current;
     const dbSnapshot = db !== undefined ? { ...localSettings, ...db } : null;
     bootstrapEntity({ entityId, db: dbSnapshot, lastUsed: localSettings });
   }, [entityId, hasPersistedData, dbUpdatedAt, bootstrapEntity]);
   ```
5. **Delete** the `hasPersistedData === (dbSnapshot !== null)` and `deepEqual(currentEntity.cleanSnapshot, nextSeed)` block at `:165-172`. Keep the preceding `reloadStoredEntity` effect at `:147-153` unchanged.

### Step 12: WP5 bootstrap idempotency + refetch test (`src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/__tests__/useLastUsedEditSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Assert** the `undefined → object` transition (first load) fires bootstrap with `db: {...}`.
2. **Assert** 5 identity-stable rerenders fire zero additional bootstraps.
3. **Assert (critical)**: a refetch — `dbUpdatedAt` advances — re-bootstraps with the new `dbSettings`.
4. **Assert** `entityId` change fires a fresh bootstrap keyed to the new entity's local seed.
5. **Assert** a change in `dbSettings` object identity without `dbUpdatedAt` change does NOT re-bootstrap.

## Phase 4: Documentation

### Step 13: Update `state_ownership.md` with `dataUpdatedAt`-keyed bootstrap rule (`docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Read** the current doc (20 lines from M7A T7). Keep Purpose + Source-of-truth table + three do-nots + `git log` pointer.
2. **Add** a new section `## Bootstrap effect key discipline` (≈10–15 lines) stating:
   - RQ result object identity MUST stay out of effect deps that call a zustand setter.
   - Scalar `dataUpdatedAt` (a `number` from React Query) IS in deps — it's the refetch key.
   - Genuine refetches (new data) re-bootstrap `cleanSnapshot`; rerender-only identity churn does not.
   - Reference implementation: the collapsed effects in `useAutoSaveSettings.ts` and `useLastUsedEditSettings.ts`.
   - Custom-mode bootstrap is entity-keyed, not content-keyed (callers that need content re-seed must change `entityId`).
3. **Verify** `wc -l docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md` < 80. Do not create a new `.md` file — extend the existing one.

## Phase 6: Verification

### Step 15: Static evidence gates (primary `must` tier in this sandbox)
**Scope:** Small
1. `rg 'isOverlayActuallyOpen' src` → empty.
2. `rg 'lastBootstrapRef' src` → empty.
3. `rg 'cleanSnapshot' src --glob '!*.test.*'` → only `createEntityStore.ts` internals.
4. `rg 'dataUpdatedAt' src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts` → ≥ 1 (new field).
5. `rg 'dataUpdatedAt' src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLastUsedEditSettings.ts` → ≥ 2 per file (destructure + dep).
6. `rg 'open:\\s*boolean' src/shared/components/ui/overlay/overlayBridge.tsx` → ≥ 1.
7. `rg "useOpenFromDataState" src/shared/components/ui/overlay` → ≥ 9 (1 definition + 8 unique wrapper import sites: dialog, alert-dialog, lightbox, select, popover, dropdown-menu, hover-card, tooltip).
8. `rg 'useOverlayBridge\\(' src/shared/components/ui/overlay/lightbox.tsx` — confirm the call is inside `LightboxDialogPopup`, not `LightboxDialogPortal` (grep for function name + bridge call order).
9. `rg 'didBootstrapRef' src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineState.ts` ≥ 2 (M7A scope firewall).
10. `rg 'usePaneLockPolicyState' src` empty (M7A scope firewall).
11. `wc -l docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md` < 80 after Step 13.
12. `rg 'Bootstrap effect key discipline' docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md` ≥ 1.
13. Record all outputs in `.megaplan/plans/m7b-architectural-rewrites/evidence_static.txt`.

### Step 16: Build + test verification (demoted to `info` in this sandbox)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Check** `ls node_modules/.bin/tsc node_modules/.bin/vitest`. If absent (expected): record the skip in the evidence file and do NOT claim success.
2. **If** the binaries are present (e.g., on a developer machine after `npm ci`): run `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx vitest run src/shared/components/ui/overlay src/shared/settings/hooks src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks src/shared/hooks/settings`, and `npm test`.
3. **Runtime smoke** (unopened `<Select>` not stranding pointer-events, lightbox entity navigation re-hydrating, dialog focus restore, same-entity genuine refetch re-bootstrapping): record as `info` priority — requires a hydrated environment / human verification post-merge.

## Execution Order
1. Phase 1 Step 1 — per-primitive classification.
2. Phase 1 Step 2 (bridge `open`) → Step 3 (helper) → Step 4 (7 wrappers) → Step 5 (lightbox restructure) → Step 6 (delete DOM-reading workaround) → Step 7 (tests).
3. Phase 2 Step 8 (`dataUpdatedAt` on `useToolSettings`) → Step 9 (collapse `useAutoSaveSettings`) → Step 10 (tests — including refetch re-bootstrap assertion).
4. Phase 3 Step 11 (port to `useLastUsedEditSettings`) → Step 12 (tests).
5. Phase 4 Step 13 — update `state_ownership.md`.
6. Phase 5 Step 14 — record `useJoinClipsSettings` deferral.
7. Phase 6 Step 15 (static gates, `must`) → Step 16 (toolchain, `info` in sandbox).

## Validation Order
1. Per-step `rg` checks as each step completes (cheap, always run).
2. After Phase 1: Step 7 overlay tests (runtime if toolchain present, else static evidence via Step 15.6–15.8).
3. After Phase 2 Step 9: `npx tsc --noEmit` if toolchain present (the `useToolSettings` return-type change ripples through every `useToolSettings` caller — compile surface settles here).
4. After Phase 3 Step 11: full `npx vitest run src/shared/settings src/domains/media-lightbox src/shared/hooks/settings` if toolchain present.
5. After Phase 4: Step 15 static-evidence sweep (always run; carries the `must` gate in sandbox).
6. Step 16 toolchain verification last; skip with documented deviation if binaries absent.
7. Runtime smoke deferred to human (info).

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 5.1 claims `updatepinnedgroupwithchildren` is 'currently imported by useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:357'. verified at useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:71-130 that this function is actually **defined locally** in useswitchtofinalvideo.ts, not imported from pinned-group-projection.ts. the real imports from pinned-group-projection at :5-8 are `buildgroupfromchildren` and `replacegroupchildren`, both of which are already in step 5's delete list. the plan's characterization is factually wrong (delete list shouldn't include updatepinnedgroupwithchildren as a pinned-group-projection export), but the substantive intent still works: once `buildgroupfromchildren`/`replacegroupchildren` are deleted, the local helper won't compile and step 11.2's 'inline soft-tag construction' rewrite removes it. minor wording error, not a blocking issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "roll-back-the-first-class-20260408-1337"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-132",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 11.2 specifies the reverse switch is 'single commit, no `start`/`children`/`offset` reads anywhere' but doesn't state the mutation type explicitly. since the step describes metadeletes + cliporderoverride + pinnedshotgroupsoverride in a single commit, the mutation must be `type: 'rows'` (the only variant carrying all three). self-consistent with step 11.1's restriction on `type: 'pinnedshotgroups'`, but making the mutation type explicit would reduce executor ambiguity.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "roll-back-the-first-class-20260408-1337"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-135",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked step 3 against the actual `useselectedmediaclips()` inputs in [useselectedmediaclips.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useselectedmediaclips.ts#l29). that hook currently reads only `selectedclipids` and `resolvedconfig` from editor context, so the plan's literal instruction to join `resolvedconfig.pinnedshotgroups` with `useshots()` data will not compile unless it first switches to `data?.config.pinnedshotgroups` or another real source of pinned groups.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-first-class-shot-20260409-2052"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-149",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual timeline selection ownership across `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelineselection.ts`, and `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usemultiselect.ts`. `usetimelinecommit` owns only `selectedclipid` and `selectedtrackid`, while `usemultiselect` still owns `selectedclipids`, `primaryclipid`, and `additiveselectionref`, so the plan's step 3 is technically incomplete: following it literally would leave timeline selection split between the new store and old hook-local state instead of collapsing it into one authoritative store.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-150",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the current imperative selection adapters in `useclipdrag.ts`, `usetimelinepersistence.ts`, `usepollsync.ts`, and `videoeditorprovider.tsx`. the mutable refs (`selectedclipidsref`, `additiveselectionref`, `selectedclipidref`, `selectedtrackidref`) are runtime adapters for drag/save/poll callers rather than durable domain state, so the plan's wording about putting 'refs/helpers' into the `timeline` slice is a design error unless it explicitly keeps store state separate from ref adapters rebuilt around that state.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-166",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelinecanvas.tsx` and `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/tracklistrenderer.tsx`. `timelinecanvas` still computes shared `rowresizepreview`, `selectedtrackid`, and other drag-sensitive props centrally and passes them through every `sortablerow`, so moving context reads to narrow zustand selectors alone will not meet the gate that non-dragged rows stay at 0 rerenders during drag unless the plan also changes this row-prop fan-out.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-167",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the staged-vs-mounted add flow in `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useaddtovideoeditor.ts`. that hook decides between immediate in-process drop and staged url/localstorage fallback by testing whether the safe timeline hooks return `null`, so a global `timelinestore.ts` with default state would change runtime behavior unless the migration preserves an explicit 'editor mounted' availability boundary.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-168",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinestate.ts` and `src/tools/video-editor/contexts/videoeditorprovider.tsx` together. the current composition is synchronous and consumers like the pending-add drain and agentchat bridge read fresh `editor` values in the same render pass, so the plan's 'build values and write them into the zustand store' step is under-specified about whether hydration happens during render or in an effect, and either choice has real correctness risk if not designed carefully.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    }

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-038",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the current callers of `isrecord` from `llm/messages.ts`. `index.ts` uses it to validate `rawsession`, `db.ts` uses it on db rows, `tool-calls.ts` uses it on parsed tool-call payloads and nested `function` objects, and `selectedclips.ts` uses it while normalizing attachments, so preserving that import contract matters to four different call paths.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "cleanup-and-quality-pass-on-20260404-0138"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-095",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the current `commitdata()` and history pipeline treat each `applyedit()` as its own commit boundary. because step 16 proposes a post-commit reconcile edit after deletions, the actual callers here matter: without `skiphistory` or transaction coalescing, one user action will produce two history events and likely two saves.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-096",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: `handleassetdrop()` and the external-drop wrapper are not pure builders; they immediately mutate editor state and call `applyedit()` per asset. that means the plan cannot literally reuse those callers for atomic shot-drop creation without first extracting a lower-level helper, or it will violate its own \"single applyedit\" requirement.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-111",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual commit/history callers around step 18. `commitdata()` emits `beforecommit` and schedules save on every edit, and `usetimelinehistory.onbeforecommit()` pushes undo entries per commit boundary, so a reconcile-after-delete `applyedit` still fragments one user action into multiple history/save operations unless the plan also changes those caller contracts.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-115",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the multi-drag callers in `useclipdrag`. the 'new track' grouped-drag branch still routes through `buildconfigfromdragresult()` and `applyedit({ type: 'config' })`, so the revised plan still does not fully handle every caller shape for grouped moves once pinned-group metadata becomes part of the timeline state.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-130",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the `usepinnedgroupsync()` call site in `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx:479` against the proposed context-based interaction guard. because sync is called directly from `timelineeditor`, routing its guard through new editor-context plumbing is not the most direct fit for the actual caller and depends on extra provider/type changes that the step does not enumerate.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [

      ]
    },

      "id": "DEBT-138",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the caller contract of `useselectedmediaclips()` via [usetimelineeditordata](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useselectedmediaclips.ts#l29). this hook's caller currently provides `selectedclipids` and `resolvedconfig`, not a resolved config object with `pinnedshotgroups`, so the plan still does not handle the actual arguments passed at that call site and needs to pull pinned groups from editor `data` instead.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-first-class-shot-20260409-2052"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-158",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual callers of the timeline-selection refs with `rg 'selectedclipidsref|additiveselectionref|selectedclipidref|selectedtrackidref' src/tools/video-editor`. save/poll paths consume `selectedclipidref` and `selectedtrackidref`, but `useclipdrag.ts`, `timelineeditor.tsx`, `videoeditorprovider.tsx`, and `usetimelinestate.contexts.ts` also consume `selectedclipidsref` and `additiveselectionref`, so the plan does not yet handle all caller shapes unless it adds a store-backed adapter for the multi-select refs as well.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]

    {
      "id": "DEBT-159",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `setselectedclipid(` callers in `useassetmanagement.ts`, `usecliptextoverlay.ts`, `propertiespanel.tsx`, and `previewpanel.tsx`. those callers pass concrete clip ids and `null` through the existing dispatch-like api, so any store action that replaces `setselectedclipid` must preserve the same semantics for clearing and reselection rather than narrowing the contract to a simpler single-purpose setter.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

      ]

      "id": "DEBT-175",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the real callers of the safe timeline hooks in `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useaddtovideoeditor.ts`. that code passes through both mounted-editor values and the `null` outside-provider case, then uses `ops.registergenerationasset`, `ops.handleassetdrop`, and `data.resolvedconfig?.clips` when available, so any replacement api has to preserve both nullable access and the current immediate-drop contract rather than only exposing non-null store selectors.",

      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [

      "id": "DEBT-177",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/videoeditorshell.tsx`, where `handlekeyboarddelete` combines `editordata.dataref.current` with `editordata.selectedclipids` to build a mutation before falling back to `handledeleteclips`. that caller needs synchronized latest-ref and latest-selection semantics, so splitting those reads across unrelated store selectors without an adapter can change behavior even if each field is individually correct.",

Read this file first — it contains 5 checks, each with a question and guidance.
For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: M7B — Architectural Rewrites (Overlay Bridge + Settings Bootstrap Collapse)

## Overview

M7A landed the mechanical cleanup slice (WP1/WP2/WP6/WP7). M7B addresses the **architectural rewrites** deferred by M7A's scope firewall: WP3 (overlay bridge `open` correctness), WP4 (collapse `useAutoSaveSettings` bootstrap effects, keyed on `dataUpdatedAt`), WP5 (port the collapse to `useLastUsedEditSettings`).

**Settled decisions carried into this revision** (from prior iterations; do not re-litigate):
- **D1** — WP4/WP5 bootstrap effects are keyed on scalar `dataUpdatedAt` (a `number`). Genuine RQ refetches DO re-bootstrap `cleanSnapshot`; identity-churn rerenders do not. Idea file requires this.
- **D2** — `useToolSettings` return shape gains one new field: `dataUpdatedAt: number`. No other shape changes.
- **D3** — Overlay Content wrappers hold the popup element via `useState<HTMLElement | null>(null)` + callback-ref, not a bare ref, so DOM attachment triggers rerender and re-invokes `useOpenFromDataState` with the real node.
- **D4** — Toolchain gates (`tsc --noEmit`, `npm test`) are `info` in this sandbox; static-evidence gates (rg/ls/wc) carry the `must` tier.
- **D5** — `useJoinClipsSettings.ts:128-195` mirror pattern is out of M7B scope — deferred to M7C, recorded in `evidence_deferred.txt`.

**Key corrections for iteration 3** (addressing critique_v2's two remaining specification gaps):

1. **Lightbox backdrop contract preserved.** Iteration 2's Step 5 moved `useOverlayBridge` from `LightboxDialogPortal` into `LightboxDialogPopup`, which orphans `LightboxDialogBackdrop` — Backdrop is a sibling of Popup under Portal, so a context provided *by* Popup cannot reach Backdrop upward. Current code at `lightbox.tsx:9-22, 33-93` shares `{ layer, registerElement }` from Portal via `LightboxDialogLayerContext`, and the existing test (`lightbox.test.tsx:34-59`) asserts both popup and backdrop register under the same overlay with layered z-index styles. The corrected approach: **keep the bridge creation in `LightboxDialogPortal`** (preserving the sibling-Backdrop contract), and extend `LightboxDialogLayerContext` with a `setPopupElement` setter. `LightboxDialogPopup` calls that setter via its `composeRefs` chain; `LightboxDialogPortal` holds `popupEl` in `useState<HTMLElement | null>(null)` and derives `open = useOpenFromDataState(popupEl)` to pass into `useOverlayBridge({ type: 'lightbox', modal, open })`. Backdrop keeps its existing `{ layer, registerElement }` consumption — no change to its file.

2. **Step 14 caller inventory split.** The caller inventory must explicitly distinguish two groups. Group A (RQ-backed via `useToolSettings`): `useCharacterAnimateSettings`, `useImageGenGallery`, `useShotSettings`, `useJoinSegmentsSettings`, `usePersistentToolState`, `useOutputSelection`, `useStructureVideo`, `useEditVideoSettings`, `useAudio`, `usePersistentPromptSettings`, `useShotManagement`, plus `useLastUsedEditSettings` itself. These callers inherit the new `dataUpdatedAt` re-bootstrap automatically — no per-caller change required. Group B (custom-mode via `customLoadSave`): `useGenerationEditSettings` (`src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useGenerationEditSettings.ts:182-199`). This caller does NOT route through `useToolSettings`; its bootstrap is entity-keyed (per D1's custom-mode branch), and the behavior is covered by Step 10's custom-mode test, not by any new integration test.

Working branch: `megaplan/m7-state-cleanup` (worktree `/private/tmp/reigh-chain-workspace/reigh-app`).

Baseline state (confirmed via inspection):
- `overlayBridge.tsx:25-30` — `UseOverlayBridgeOptions` has no `open`; `syncRegistration` at `:136-161` only pops on zero elements.
- `overlayStack.ts:88-101` — `isOverlayActuallyOpen` DOM-reading workaround still present; `getTopModalOverlay` at `:103-112` still calls it.
- 8 Content wrapper `useOverlayBridge({...})` call sites: dialog `:68`, alert-dialog `:56`, lightbox `:38` (inside `LightboxDialogPortal`), select `:198`, popover `:51`, dropdown-menu `:140` + `:83`, hover-card `:40`, tooltip `:49`.
- `lightbox.tsx:14-22` — `LightboxDialogLayerContext` provides `{ layer, registerElement }` to both `LightboxDialogBackdrop` (`:56-73`) and `LightboxDialogPopup` (`:76-93`) as siblings.
- `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:236-240` — `lastBootstrapRef`; two bootstrap effects at `:280-307` (custom-mode) and `:309-359` (RQ-mode); RQ-mode effect deps include `authoritativeSettings` (the violation).
- `useLastUsedEditSettings.ts:155-179` — effect deps include `dbSettings`; deepEqual guard at `:165-172`.
- `useToolSettings` returns `settings: T | undefined`; `dataUpdatedAt` not surfaced today.
- Toolchain: `node_modules/@base-ui/react` empty; `tsc`/`vitest` binaries absent (same as M7A baseline).
- `docs/structure_detail/state_ownership.md` exists (20 lines from M7A T7).

Invariants M7B enforces:
1. **Scalar `dataUpdatedAt` IS in deps; RQ result object identity IS NOT.** Refs carry the RQ result; effect body reads via ref.
2. **`open` is required on `UseOverlayBridgeOptions`.** Each bridge caller passes a boolean derived from `useOpenFromDataState(popupEl)` where `popupEl` is held in `useState`.
3. **Lightbox structure preserved**: bridge stays in Portal; Portal holds the popup element state; Popup feeds it via a new `setPopupElement` context setter; Backdrop is unchanged.
4. **Scope firewall**: do not touch M7A's landed surface (`usePaneLockPolicyState` deletion, `didBootstrapRef`, diagnostic log strip, inlined panes bootstrap). Do not touch `useJoinClipsSettings` in M7B.
5. **`useToolSettings` API change is scoped to adding `dataUpdatedAt: number`.** Nothing else.

## Phase 1: Overlay Bridge Correction (WP3)

### Step 1: Per-primitive classification of the 8 overlay wrappers
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** `ls node_modules/@base-ui/react` and record the output (expected: empty directory matching M7A baseline).
2. **Do not** run `npm install` (sandbox lacks network).

   | Wrapper file | Root wrapper location | Root exposes controlled `open`? | `useXRootContext` reachable? | Final classification |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | `dialog.tsx` | `:16` | No (uncontrolled) | No (empty package) | (c) MutationObserver |
   | `alert-dialog.tsx` | top of file | No | No | (c) MutationObserver |

   | `select.tsx` | `:19-29` | Root exposes `open`/`onOpenChange` props but app doesn't drive them | No | (c) MutationObserver |
   | `popover.tsx` | `:10-17` | Same (not driven) | No | (c) MutationObserver |
   | `dropdown-menu.tsx` | `:13-20` | Same (not driven) | No | (c) MutationObserver |
   | `hover-card.tsx` | wraps PreviewCardPrimitive | No | No | (c) MutationObserver |
   | `tooltip.tsx` | wraps TooltipPrimitive | No | No | (c) MutationObserver |
4. All 8 resolve to (c). No executor deviation required — this is the primary, idea-file-compliant path.

### Step 2: Add `open` as required to `useOverlayBridge`, gate registration (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/overlayBridge.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `open: boolean` as required on `UseOverlayBridgeOptions` at `:25-30`.
2. **Track** `open` via a ref updated every render:
   ```ts
   const openRef = React.useRef(options.open);
   openRef.current = options.open;
   ```
3. **Modify** `syncRegistration` at `:136-161`: when `!openRef.current || elements.length === 0`, pop-if-registered and return.
4. **Include** `options.open` in the deps of the `useEffect` at `:186-188` that re-fires `syncRegistration`, so `open` transitions trigger a pop.
5. **Leave** unmount cleanup at `:190-204` untouched.

### Step 3: Create the shared `useOpenFromDataState` helper (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/useOpenFromDataState.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** the file with:
   ```tsx
   import * as React from 'react';

   export function useOpenFromDataState(node: HTMLElement | null): boolean {
     const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
     React.useEffect(() => {
       if (!node) { setOpen(false); return; }
       const read = () => {
         const state = node.getAttribute('data-state');
         setOpen(state === 'open' || node.hasAttribute('data-open'));
       };
       read();
       const mo = new MutationObserver(read);
       mo.observe(node, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-state', 'data-open'] });
       return () => mo.disconnect();
     }, [node]);
     return open;
   }
   ```
   The caller holds `node` in `useState` (per D3), so DOM attachment triggers rerender and this effect re-subscribes with the real node.

### Step 4: Wire 7 local-popup Content wrappers to self-source `open` (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/{dialog,alert-dialog,select,popover,dropdown-menu,hover-card,tooltip}.tsx`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Pattern for each wrapper** (DialogContent, AlertDialogContent, SelectContent, PopoverContent, DropdownMenuContent, DropdownMenuSubContent, HoverCardContent, TooltipContent):
   ```tsx
   const [popupEl, setPopupEl] = React.useState<HTMLElement | null>(null);
   const open = useOpenFromDataState(popupEl);
   const bridge = useOverlayBridge({ type, modal, open });
   const layer = useOverlayLayer(bridge.handle.id);
   const popupRef = React.useMemo(
     () => composeRefs(
       ref,
       (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => setPopupEl(node),
       (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => bridge.registerElement('popup', node),
     ),
     [bridge, ref],
   );
   ```
2. **Apply** at: dialog.tsx `:68`, alert-dialog.tsx `:56`, select.tsx `:198`, popover.tsx `:51`, dropdown-menu.tsx `:140` + `:83`, hover-card.tsx `:40`, tooltip.tsx `:49`.
3. **Preserve** all existing `modal` derivation from context (SelectModalContext, PopoverModalContext, DropdownMenuModalContext).
4. **Do NOT modify** callers of the Root aliases (`<Dialog>`, `<Popover>`, `<Tooltip>`, etc.) outside `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/`.

### Step 5: Lightbox — keep bridge in Portal; thread popup element upward via extended LayerContext (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/lightbox.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Inspect** `lightbox.tsx:9-22, 33-93`. The bridge and `LightboxDialogLayerContext` live in `LightboxDialogPortal`; `LightboxDialogBackdrop` (`:56-73`) and `LightboxDialogPopup` (`:76-93`) are **siblings** that both consume `{ layer, registerElement }` from the context. The lightbox test (`lightbox.test.tsx:34-59`) asserts both register under the same overlay.
2. **Extend** `LightboxDialogLayerContextValue` with a third field:
   ```ts
   interface LightboxDialogLayerContextValue {
     layer: number | null;
     registerElement: ReturnType<typeof useOverlayBridge>['registerElement'];
     setPopupElement: (node: HTMLElement | null) => void;
   }
   ```
3. **In `LightboxDialogPortal`**: add `const [popupEl, setPopupEl] = React.useState<HTMLElement | null>(null)` and `const open = useOpenFromDataState(popupEl)` **before** the `useOverlayBridge({ type: 'lightbox', modal, open })` call. Include `setPopupElement: setPopupEl` in the `useMemo` that builds `contextValue`. This keeps the bridge creation at Portal (Backdrop's sibling-consumption stays intact) while making `open` observable from below.
4. **In `LightboxDialogPopup`**: destructure `setPopupElement` from `useLightboxDialogLayerContext()` and add it to the `composeRefs` chain so attaching the popup DOM node invokes it:
   ```tsx
   const { layer, registerElement, setPopupElement } = useLightboxDialogLayerContext();
   const popupRef = React.useMemo(
     () => composeRefs(
       ref,
       (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => setPopupElement(node),
       (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => registerElement('popup', node),
     ),
     [ref, registerElement, setPopupElement],
   );
   ```
5. **`LightboxDialogBackdrop` is NOT modified.** It still consumes `{ layer, registerElement }` from the same `LightboxDialogLayerContext` — the additional `setPopupElement` field is ignored by it. Existing `registerElement('backdrop', node)` wiring at `:62` stays.
6. **Public export surface** (`<LightboxDialog>`, `<LightboxDialogPortal>`, `<LightboxDialogBackdrop>`, `<LightboxDialogPopup>`, `<LightboxDialogTitle>`, `<LightboxDialogDescription>`) is unchanged.
7. **Re-verify** `lightbox.test.tsx` still passes logically: the top overlay still contains both popup and backdrop (because the bridge is still in Portal and both still call `registerElement`), and both still receive layered z-index styles from the shared `layer`. Update a test only if a specific assertion depended on the Portal bridge being the one observing `open`.
